### The Portugal Tree ###

focus_tree = {
	id = portuguese_focus
	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			tag = POR
		}
	}
	##############################
	###	Start of EU Focus Tree ###	x = 120 y = 15
	##############################
	shared_focus = USoE001
	shared_focus = POTEF001
	############################
	### End of EU Focus Tree ###
	############################
	continuous_focus_position = { x = 350 y = 2300 }

	shortcut = {
		name = POR_politics
		target = POR_politics
		scroll_wheel_factor = 0.80
	}
	shortcut = {
		name = POR_departments
		target = POR_departments

	}

	#### ECONOMY ####

	#Portugal Economy
	focus = {
		id = POR_economy
		icon = POR_economy

		x = 20
		y = 5

		cost = 4.5

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_INDUSTRY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus POR_economy"
			increase_economic_growth = yes
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 10.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	#Industrial Renewal Program
	focus = {
		id = POR_industrial_renewal
		icon = BLR_development_plan

		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = POR_economy

		cost = 4

		prerequisite = { focus = POR_economy }
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_INDUSTRY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus POR_industrial_renewal"
			one_random_industrial_complex = yes
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = CAT_industry
				bonus = 0.15
				uses = 1
				category = CAT_industry
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 75
		}
	}

	#Volkswagen Autoeuropa
	focus = {
		id = POR_volkswagen_autoeuropa
		icon = volkswagen

		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = POR_salvador_caetano

		cost = 5

		prerequisite = { focus = POR_salvador_caetano }
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_INDUSTRY }

		available = {
			has_opinion = {
				target = GER
				value > 49
			}
			NOT = { has_war_with = GER }
		}

		bypass = {
			NOT = { country_exists = GER }
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus POR_volkswagen_autoeuropa"
			GER = { country_event = { id = portugal_economy.1 days = 1 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_if_they_accept_TT
			effect_tooltip = {
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1.5 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = GER }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = POR }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = GER
					modifier = hungary_diplomatic_partnership
				}
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = POR
					modifier = hungary_diplomatic_partnership
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 75
		}
	}

				limit = { has_idea = farmers }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
				change_farmers_opinion = yes
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_idea = landowners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = landowners
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = industrial_conglomerates }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = industrial_conglomerates
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = oligarchs }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = oligarchs
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = international_bankers }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = international_bankers
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = maritime_industry }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = maritime_industry
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = the_clergy }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = the_clergy
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = intelligence_community
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = small_medium_business_owners
						add_idea = farmers
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
			change_farmers_opinion = yes
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = POR_proletarian_spirit_idea
				add_idea = POR_proletarian_spirit1_idea

					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = industrial_conglomerates
						add_idea = international_bankers
					}

						add_idea = the_military

						add_idea = international_bankers
					}
				}

					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = the_clergy
						add_idea = international_bankers
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = intelligence_community
						add_idea = international_bankers
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes

				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = maritime_industry }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = maritime_industry
						add_idea = the_military
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = the_clergy }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = the_clergy
						add_idea = the_military
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = intelligence_community
						add_idea = the_military
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = small_medium_business_owners
						add_idea = the_military
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 15 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
			add_command_power = 25
			army_experience = 25
			add_doctrine_cost_reduction = {
				name = CAT_land_doctrine
				cost_reduction = 0.5
				uses = 2
				category = CAT_land_doctrine
			}
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = GENERIC_army_reform_up_idea
				days = 300
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 50
		}
	}
